# amex-archives — Repository Index

> Auto-generated index of every repository in the AmEx internal GitHub Enterprise archive org: <https://github.aexp.com/amex-archives>
> This org is a general enterprise-wide archive (retired/decommissioned repos across all business domains), not architecture-specific material. Repos are **not** cloned locally by default — this file is a lookup index only. If you need the contents of a specific repo, clone it on request: `git clone https://github.aexp.com/amex-archives/<name>.git`.

**Generated:** 2026-09-14
**Total repositories:** 2038
**Total size:** ~92.04 GB

> **Note on contributors/owners:** the GitHub Enterprise repo-listing API does not include per-repo contributor data without an additional API call per repo (2,038 calls would be needed — impractical to run in bulk). All repos show `owner: amex-archives` because that's the archive org they now live under, not their original team. If you need contributor history for a specific repo, ask and it can be fetched (or check that repo's own commit log / `git log`) on demand.
